/*# sourceURL=searchwp-live-search-inline-css */ </style> <style id='fkcart-style-inline-css' type='text/css'> :root {
 --fkcart-primary-bg-color: #6a8357;
 --fkcart-primary-font-color: #ffffff;
 --fkcart-primary-text-color: #353030;
 --fkcart-secondary-text-color: #82838E;
 --fkcart-strike-through-price-text-color: #E15334;
 --fkcart-saving-text-price-color: #5BA238;
 --fkcart-coupon-text-price-color: #5BA238;
 --fkcart-accent-color: #6a8357;
 --fkcart-border-color: #6a8357;
 --fkcart-error-color: #B00C0C;

 const paypalBtns = document.querySelectorAll('.ppc-button-wrapper button, .ppc-button-wrapper input[type="submit"]');
 paypalBtns.forEach(btn => {

 updateButtons();
 // Run again after 1 second to catch PayPal dynamic rendering
 setTimeout(updateButtons, 1200);

.popup-overlay {
 position: fixed;
 top:0;
 left:0;
 width:100%;
 height:100%;
 background: rgba(0,0,0,0.7);
 z-index: 9999;
 display: none;
 justify-content: center;
 align-items: center;
}
.popup-content {
 background:#fff;
 padding:30px 25px;
 max-width:500px;
 width:100%;
 border-radius:10px;
 position:relative;
 text-align:left;
}
@media (max-width: 600px) {
 .popup-content {
 max-height: 85vh !important;
 overflow-y: auto !important;
 margin: 12px 10px 12px 10px !important; /* top margin 70px, left/right 10px */
 }
}
.close-btn {
 position:absolute;
 top:15px;
 right:20px;
 font-size:26px;
 font-weight:bold;
 cursor:pointer;
}
.popup-content h2{text-align:center; font-size:24px;}
.popup-content p{font-size:15px; margin-bottom:12px;}
.terms {margin-top:15px; font-size:14px;}
.terms a{color:#0073aa; text-decoration:underline;}
.terms strong{font-weight:bold;}
.popup-buttons{display:flex; justify-content:space-between; margin-top:20px; gap:10px;}
#popup-exit-btn{flex:1; padding:12px 0; background:#ccc; border:none; border-radius:6px; cursor:pointer;}
#popup-agree-btn{flex:1; padding:12px 0; background:#0073aa; color:#fff; border:none; border-radius:6px; cursor:pointer;}
#popup-agree-btn:disabled{background:#999; cursor:not-allowed;}
/* Product overlay */
.lock-overlay{
 position:absolute;
 top:0;
 left:0;
 width:100%;
 height:100%;
 background: rgba(255,255,255,0.8);
 display:flex;
 flex-direction:column;
 justify-content:center;
 align-items:center;
 text-align:center;
 z-index:999;
}
.lock-overlay .lock-icon{font-size:30px; margin-bottom:10px;}
.lock-overlay .login-btn{margin-top:10px; padding:8px 15px; background:#0073aa; color:#fff; border-radius:5px; text-decoration:none;}
/* Mobile adjustments */
@media(max-width:600px){
 .popup-content{max-height:85vh; overflow-y:auto; margin:50px 10px;}
} </style> <!-- Popup HTML --><div id="age-verification-popup" class="popup-overlay"><div class="popup-content"> <span class="close-btn">×</span><h2>Age Verification Required</h2><p>This website is restricted to approved members of the REVIVA | CORE Collective. To continue, please confirm you meet the minimum age requirement and accept the agreements below.</p><p class="terms"><label><input type="checkbox" id="checkbox-age"> <strong>I confirm I am 21+ years of age or older.</strong></label></p><p class="terms"><label><input type="checkbox" id="checkbox-terms"> I acknowledge that products and information on this website are provided for laboratory research use only and are not intended for use in or on humans or animals. I agree not to use any products or information from this website for diagnosis, treatment, cure, or prevention of any condition. I agree to comply with all applicable laws and regulations, a and I agree to the <a href="https://revivacore.com/terms-of-service/" target="_blank"><strong>Terms of Service</strong></a> and <a href="https://revivacore.com/privacy-policy/" target="_blank"><strong>Privacy Policy</strong></a>.</label></p><p>By clicking <strong>I Agree &amp; Enter</strong>, you confirm the statements above and acknowledge responsibility for compliance with applicable laws and regulations.</p><div class="popup-buttons"> <button id="popup-exit-btn">Exit</button> <button id="popup-agree-btn" disabled>I Agree &amp; Enter</button></div></div></div> <script> document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
 const popup = document.getElementById('age-verification-popup');
 const checkboxAge = document.getElementById('checkbox-age');
 const checkboxTerms = document.getElementById('checkbox-terms');
 const agreeBtn = document.getElementById('popup-agree-btn');
 const exitBtn = document.getElementById('popup-exit-btn');
 const closeBtn = document.querySelector('.close-btn');
 // Check if user confirmed popup
// Show popup only on homepage
if(localStorage.getItem('ageVerified') !== 'yes' && !document.body.classList.contains('woocommerce-shop')){
 popup.style.display = 'flex';
}  function checkCheckboxes(){
 agreeBtn.disabled = !(checkboxAge.checked && checkboxTerms.checked);
 }
 checkboxAge.addEventListener('change', checkCheckboxes);
 checkboxTerms.addEventListener('change', checkCheckboxes);
 exitBtn.addEventListener('click', ()=> window.location.href="/");
 closeBtn.addEventListener('click', ()=> window.location.href="/"); 
agreeBtn.addEventListener('click', ()=>{
 if(!agreeBtn.disabled){
 localStorage.setItem('ageVerified','yes');
 window.location.href="/"; // "/" হলো homepage
 }
});
 popup.addEventListener('click', function(e){
 if(e.target===popup) window.location.href="/";
 });
 // Function to add product overlay if user not logged in
 function addProductLockOverlay(){
 const products = document.querySelectorAll('.products .product');
 const loggedIn = document.body.classList.contains('logged-in'); // WP body class
 if(loggedIn) return;
 products.forEach(product=>{
 if(!product.querySelector('.lock-overlay')){
 const overlay=document.createElement('div');
 overlay.className='lock-overlay';
 overlay.innerHTML=`<span class="lock-icon">&#128274;</span><p>Please login to view products</p><a href="/my-account" class="login-btn">Login</a>`;
 product.style.position='relative';
 product.appendChild(overlay);
 }
 });
 }
 // Initial call
 addProductLockOverlay();
 // Re-run after AJAX filter
 jQuery(document).ajaxComplete(function(){ addProductLockOverlay(); });
}); </script><script defer type="text/javascript" src="https://revivacore.com/wp-includes/js/dist/vendor/react.min.js?ver=18.3.1.1" id="react-js"></script> <script defer type="text/javascript" src="https://revivacore.com/wp-includes/js/dist/vendor/react-jsx-runtime.min.js?ver=18.3.1" id="react-jsx-runtime-js"></script> <script type="text/javascript" src="https://revivacore.com/wp-includes/js/dist/vendor/wp-polyfill.min.js?ver=3.15.0" id="wp-polyfill-js"></script> <script defer type="text/javascript" src="https://revivacore.com/wp-content/uploads/siteground-optimizer-assets/wc-types.min.js?ver=f0cc692c0367e0930c80" id="wc-types-js"></script> <script type="text/javascript" src="https://revivacore.com/wp-includes/js/dist/hooks.min.js?ver=dd5603f07f9220ed27f1" id="wp-hooks-js"></script> <script type="text/javascript" src="https://revivacore.com/wp-includes/js/dist/i18n.min.js?ver=c26c3dc7bed366793375" id="wp-i18n-js"></script> <script type="text/javascript" id="wp-i18n-js-after"> /* <![CDATA[ */
